[+   1.834s] [host] diff| +/* ---- T2 on SPI1: register images against sst_t2_expect() ------------------------------- */

[+   1.838s] [host] diff| +/* ---- Transfers on SPI1: polling, interrupt, DMA2 ----------------------------------- */
[+   1.838s] [host] diff| +static SpiBm_Xfer_t      s_it;
[+   1.838s] [host] diff| +static volatile bool     s_it_active;
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +static volatile uint32_t s_it_irqs;
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +static volatile uint32_t s_it_spurious;
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t          s_b1_irq_min = 0xFFFFFFFFUL;

[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +/* The project's vector for the test (plan C14), CPU2's NVIC, priority 6. */
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +void SPI1_IRQHandler(void)
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +    s_it_irqs++;

[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +        const bool done = SPI_BM_TransferIrq(SPI1, &s_it);
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t dt = DWT->CYCCNT - t0;
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +        if (dt < s_b1_irq_min)
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +            s_b1_irq_min = dt;
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +        if (dt > s_b1_irq_max)
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +            s_b1_irq_max = dt;
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +        if (done)
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +            s_it_active = false;
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +    else
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +        s_it_spurious++;
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +        SPI1->IER = 0U;
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +}

[+   1.840s] [host] diff| +static bool m4_stream(DMA_Stream_TypeDef *s, bool tx, uint32_t es, void *buf, uint32_t n)
[+   1.840s] [host] diff| +{

[+   1.840s] [host] diff| +        .base = { .stream = s, .request_id = req, .direction = tx ? DMA_BM_DIR_MEM_TO_PERIPH : DMA_BM_DIR_PERIPH_TO_MEM,

[+   1.859s] [host] diff| +/* Reset values typed from RM0399 53.11 (CR1 53:3188, CR2 53:3285, CFG1 53:3309, CFG2

[+   1.859s] [host] diff| +static const uint32_t k_reset_regs[NREG] = { 0U, 0U, 0x00070007UL, 0U, 0U, 0x00000107UL, 0U, 0U };
[+   1.859s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.859s] [host] diff| +/* Bit k set = register k differs from its reset value (T14's oracle). */
[+   1.859s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t reset_diff(const SPI_TypeDef *s)
[+   1.859s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.859s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t r[NREG];
[+   1.859s] [host] diff| +    spi_regs(s, r);
[+   1.859s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t d = 0U;
[+   1.859s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t k = 0U; k < NREG; k++)
[+   1.859s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.859s] [host] diff| +        if (r[k] != k_reset_regs[k])
[+   1.859s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.859s] [host] diff| +            d |= 1UL << k;
[+   1.859s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.859s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.859s] [host] diff| +    return d;
[+   1.859s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.859s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.859s] [host] diff| +/* T14 for one instance: Deinit, then the reset image. The control (`ctrl`) runs the
[+   1.859s] [host] diff| +   same comparison on the instance still initialised, which must differ. */
[+   1.859s] [host] diff| +static void t14_check(const char *tag, SPI_TypeDef *spi, const SpiBm_Config_t *ctrl)
[+   1.859s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.859s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t dctrl = 0U;
[+   1.859s] [host] diff| +    if (ctrl != NULL)
[+   1.859s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.859s] [host] diff| +        (void)SPI_BM_Init(ctrl);
[+   1.860s] [host] diff| +        dctrl = reset_diff(spi);
[+   1.860s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.860s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t st = SPI_BM_Deinit(spi);
[+   1.860s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t d = reset_diff(spi);
[+   1.860s] [host] diff| +    verdict((st == SPI_BM_OK) && (d == 0U) && ((ctrl == NULL) || (dctrl != 0U)),
[+   1.860s] [host] diff| +            "T14 %s Deinit %u, registers that differ from the RM0399 reset image 0x%02lX (0); control, still "
[+   1.860s] [host] diff| +            "initialised: 0x%02lX%s", tag, (unsigned)st, (unsigned long)d, (unsigned long)dctrl,
[+   1.860s] [host] diff| +            (ctrl == NULL) ? " (no control)" : " (!= 0)");
[+   1.860s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.860s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.860s] [host] diff| +/* ---- T10: the kernel clock field, against RCC read directly ---------------------------- */
[+   1.860s] [host] diff| +static void f1_t10(void)
[+   1.860s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.860s] [host] diff| +    SPI_TypeDef *const inst[6] = { SPI1, SPI2, SPI3, SPI4, SPI5, SPI6 };
[+   1.860s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t d2 = RCC->D2CCIP1R;
[+   1.860s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t d3 = RCC->D3CCIPR;
[+   1.860s] [host] diff| +    /* Field positions typed from RM0399 9.7.19 (SPI123SEL 14:12, SPI45SEL 18:16) and
[+   1.860s] [host] diff| +       9.7.21 (SPI6SEL 30:28). */
[+   1.860s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t raw[6] = { (d2 >> 12) & 7U, (d2 >> 12) & 7U, (d2 >> 12) & 7U, (d2 >> 16) & 7U,
[+   1.860s] [host] diff| +                              (d2 >> 16) & 7U, (d3 >> 28) & 7U };
[+   1.860s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t bad = 0U;
[+   1.860s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t got[6] = { 9U, 9U, 9U, 9U, 9U, 9U };
[+   1.860s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < 6U; i++)
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +        if ((SPI_BM_GetKernelSource(inst[i], &got[i]) != SPI_BM_OK) || (got[i] != raw[i]) || (got[i] != 0U))
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +            bad |= 1UL << i;
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +    verdict(bad == 0U, "T10 GetKernelSource SPI1..6 = %lu %lu %lu %lu %lu %lu; RCC_D2CCIP1R 0x%08lX RCC_D3CCIPR 0x%08lX "
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +            "read directly give the same (0 in prj_01): differ 0x%02lX", (unsigned long)got[0], (unsigned long)got[1],
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)got[2], (unsigned long)got[3], (unsigned long)got[4], (unsigned long)got[5],
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)d2, (unsigned long)d3, (unsigned long)bad);
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +/* ---- T3: the pure functions, printed for the host to recompute (C15) ------------------- */
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t s_b1_calc_max;     /* B1: worst CalcPrescaler, cycles */
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +static void t3_p(uint32_t *i, uint32_t k, uint32_t x)
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t mbr = 0xFFU;
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t sck = 0U;
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t st = SPI_BM_CalcPrescaler(k, x, &mbr, &sck);
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t dt = DWT->CYCCNT - t0;
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +    if (dt > s_b1_calc_max)
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +        s_b1_calc_max = dt;
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +    LOG("T3P %lu k=%lu x=%lu st=%u mbr=%lu sck=%lu", (unsigned long)*i, (unsigned long)k, (unsigned long)x,
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +        (unsigned)st, (unsigned long)((st == SPI_BM_OK) ? mbr : 0U), (unsigned long)((st == SPI_BM_OK) ? sck : 0U));
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +    (*i)++;
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +static void t3_g(uint32_t *i, uint32_t c, uint32_t k, uint32_t m)
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t cyc = 0U;
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t st = SPI_BM_CalcGuardCycles(c, k, m, &cyc);
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +    LOG("T3G %lu c=%lu k=%lu m=%lu st=%u cyc=%lu", (unsigned long)*i, (unsigned long)c, (unsigned long)k,
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +        (unsigned long)m, (unsigned)st, (unsigned long)((st == SPI_BM_OK) ? cyc : 0U));
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +    (*i)++;
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.862s] [host] diff| +static void f1_t3(void)
[+   1.863s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.863s] [host] diff| +    static const uint32_t ks[] = { 1U, 2U, 3U, 255U, 256U, 257U, 511U, 512U, 513U, 1000000U, 99999989U,
[+   1.863s] [host] diff| +                                   100000000U, 101250000U, 200000000U, 202600000U, 268435456U, 2147483647U,
[+   1.863s] [host] diff| +                                   2147483648U, 4294967295U };
[+   1.863s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t n = 0U;
[+   1.863s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t a = 0U; a < (sizeof(ks) / sizeof(ks[0])); a++)
[+   1.863s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.863s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t k = ks[a];
[+   1.863s] [host] diff| +        /* the limits of the rule: max = k/2, k/256 (exact, and one below), their
[+   1.863s] [host] diff| +           neighbours, 1, k itself, k + 1, the bench speeds and a small prime */
[+   1.863s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t xs[11] = { k / 2U, (k / 2U) - 1U, k / 256U, (k / 256U) - 1U, (k / 256U) + 1U, 1U, k,
[+   1.863s] [host] diff| +                                  (k == 0xFFFFFFFFUL) ? k : (k + 1U), 25000000U, 12500000U, 7U };
[+   1.863s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t b = 0U; b < 11U; b++)
[+   1.863s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +            if (xs[b] != 0U)
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +                t3_p(&n, k, xs[b]);
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    t3_p(&n, 0U, 1U);
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    t3_p(&n, 1U, 0U);
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    t3_p(&n, 0U, 0U);
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t np = n;
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    static const uint32_t cs[] = { 1U, 64000000U, 200000000U, 400000000U, 405200000U, 4294967295U };
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    static const uint32_t kk[] = { 1U, 3U, 100000000U, 200000000U, 4294967295U };
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t g = 0U;
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t a = 0U; a < (sizeof(cs) / sizeof(cs[0])); a++)
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t b = 0U; b < (sizeof(kk) / sizeof(kk[0])); b++)
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +            t3_g(&g, cs[a], kk[b], 0U);
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +            t3_g(&g, cs[a], kk[b], 3U);
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +            t3_g(&g, cs[a], kk[b], 7U);
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t m = 0U; m <= 8U; m++)
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +        t3_g(&g, 400000000U, 100000000U, m);       /* the bench's SPI4-6; m = 8 refused */
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    t3_g(&g, 0U, 1U, 0U);
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    t3_g(&g, 1U, 0U, 0U);
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    t3_g(&g, 4294967295U, 1U, 7U);                 /* 2^40 cycles: ERR_RANGE */
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    LOG("T3 done: %lu CalcPrescaler vectors, %lu CalcGuardCycles vectors (the host recomputes them)",
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +        (unsigned long)np, (unsigned long)g);
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +/* ---- T1: refusals, with the registers of the instances and both RCC banks read
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +   before and after (C6, C12). The expected status of each case is written by hand. ---- */
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +#define T1_MAX        64U
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +static uint8_t  s_t1_got[T1_MAX];
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +static uint8_t  s_t1_exp[T1_MAX];
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t s_t1_n;
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +static void t1(SpiBm_Status_t got, SpiBm_Status_t exp)
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    if (s_t1_n < T1_MAX)
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +        s_t1_got[s_t1_n] = (uint8_t)got;
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +        s_t1_exp[s_t1_n] = (uint8_t)exp;
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    s_t1_n++;
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +#define T1_SNAP       ((4U * NREG) + 6U)
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +static void t1_snap(uint32_t *r)
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    SPI_TypeDef *const inst[4] = { SPI2, SPI4, SPI5, SPI6 };
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < 4U; i++)
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +        spi_regs(inst[i], &r[i * NREG]);
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    r[32] = RCC_C1->APB1LENR;
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    r[33] = RCC_C1->APB2ENR;
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    r[34] = RCC_C1->APB4ENR;
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    r[35] = RCC_C2->APB1LENR;
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    r[36] = RCC_C2->APB2ENR;
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    r[37] = RCC_C2->APB4ENR;
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +static void f1_t1(void)
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    SPI_TypeDef *const inst[4] = { SPI2, SPI4, SPI5, SPI6 };
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < 4U; i++)
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +        (void)SPI_BM_EnableClock(inst[i]);
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +        (void)SPI_BM_Deinit(inst[i]);
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t a[T1_SNAP];
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t b[T1_SNAP];
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t same = 0U;
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Config_t c = mkcfg(SPI2, 7U, 0U, 8U, false, SPI_BM_DIR_FULL_DUPLEX, 1U);
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    SpiBm_Config_t k;
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t v = 0U;
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t w = 0U;
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    IRQn_Type irq = SPI2_IRQn;
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    SpiBm_Caps_t caps;
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    s_t1_n = 0U;
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    /* 1. Nothing initialised: parameters. */
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    t1_snap(a);
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_BM_CheckConfig(NULL), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_BM_Init(NULL), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    k = c; k.spi = (SPI_TypeDef *)USART3;                         t1(SPI_BM_Init(&k), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    k = c; k.spi = (SPI_TypeDef *)((uint32_t)SPI1 + 4U);          t1(SPI_BM_Init(&k), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    k = c; k.data_bits = 3U;                                       t1(SPI_BM_Init(&k), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    k = c; k.data_bits = 33U;                                      t1(SPI_BM_Init(&k), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    k = c; k.spi = SPI4; k.data_bits = 17U;                        t1(SPI_BM_Init(&k), SPI_BM_ERR_UNSUPPORTED);
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    k = c; k.spi = SPI5; k.data_bits = 17U;                        t1(SPI_BM_Init(&k), SPI_BM_ERR_UNSUPPORTED);
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    k = c; k.spi = SPI6; k.data_bits = 17U;                        t1(SPI_BM_Init(&k), SPI_BM_ERR_UNSUPPORTED);
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    k = c; k.mode = 4U;                                            t1(SPI_BM_Init(&k), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    k = c; k.mbr = 8U;                                             t1(SPI_BM_Init(&k), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    k = c; k.direction = 2U;                                       t1(SPI_BM_Init(&k), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +    k = c; k.nss = 3U;                                             t1(SPI_BM_Init(&k), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +    k = c; k.nss = SPI_BM_NSS_HW; k.mssi = 16U;                    t1(SPI_BM_Init(&k), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +    k = c; k.midi = 16U;                                           t1(SPI_BM_Init(&k), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +    k = c; k.mssi = 1U;                                            t1(SPI_BM_Init(&k), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +    k = c; k.fifo_threshold = 0U;                                  t1(SPI_BM_Init(&k), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +    k = c; k.fifo_threshold = 17U;                                 t1(SPI_BM_Init(&k), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +    k = c; k.spi = SPI4; k.data_bits = 16U; k.fifo_threshold = 3U; t1(SPI_BM_Init(&k), SPI_BM_ERR_RANGE);
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +    k = c; k.data_bits = 32U; k.fifo_threshold = 3U;               t1(SPI_BM_Init(&k), SPI_BM_ERR_RANGE);
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_BM_TransferStartIt(SPI2, NULL), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_BM_CalcPrescaler(100U, 1U, NULL, &v), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_BM_CalcGuardCycles(1U, 1U, 8U, &v), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);

[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_BM_GetDmaRequest(SPI2, true, NULL), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_BM_EnableClock((SPI_TypeDef *)USART3), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_BM_DisableClock(NULL), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_BM_GetKernelSource(SPI2, NULL), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_BM_Deinit((SPI_TypeDef *)USART3), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_BM_DmaStart(NULL), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    t1_snap(b);
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    same |= (memcmp(a, b, sizeof(a)) == 0) ? 1U : 0U;
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    /* 2. SPI2 transmit only, SPI5 16-bit: the transfer's own parameters. */
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    k = c; k.direction = SPI_BM_DIR_TX_ONLY;
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t i2 = SPI_BM_Init(&k);
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    k = c; k.spi = SPI5; k.data_bits = 16U;
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t i5 = SPI_BM_Init(&k);
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    SpiBm_Xfer_t x = { s_tx, NULL, 1U, 0U, 0U, 0U, SPI_BM_OK };
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    t1_snap(a);
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_BM_Transfer(SPI2, s_tx, NULL, 0U, 1U, 1000U), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_BM_Transfer(SPI2, s_tx, NULL, 65536U, 1U, 1000U), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_BM_Transfer(SPI2, NULL, NULL, 1U, 1U, 1000U), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_BM_Transfer(SPI2, s_tx, NULL, 1U, 0U, 1000U), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_BM_Transfer(SPI2, s_tx, NULL, 1U, 1U, 0U), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_BM_Transfer(SPI2, s_tx, s_rx, 1U, 1U, 1000U), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);          /* rx in TX_ONLY */
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_BM_Transfer(SPI5, (const uint8_t *)s_tx + 1U, NULL, 1U, 1U, 1000U), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);   /* odd */
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_BM_TransferStartIt(SPI2, &x), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);                             /* guard 0 */
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_BM_DmaPrepare(SPI2, 1U, true), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);                            /* rx in TX_ONLY */
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_BM_DmaPrepare(SPI2, 0U, false), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_BM_DmaPrepare(SPI2, 65536U, false), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_BM_DmaEnd(SPI2, 0U), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);

[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +static void t2_one(const SpiBm_Config_t *c, T2Count_t *t)
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +{

[+   1.875s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)e2, (unsigned long)gcr, (unsigned long)ecr1);
[+   1.875s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.875s] [host] diff| +        t->bad++;

[+   1.885s] [host] diff| +/* ---- T9: the engine, without pins: t(2000) - t(1000) in CPU cycles ------------------ */
[+   1.885s] [host] diff| +static void t9_one(SPI_TypeDef *spi, const char *name, uint32_t mbr, uint32_t bits, uint32_t *bad)
[+   1.885s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.885s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Config_t c = mkcfg(spi, mbr, 0U, bits, false, SPI_BM_DIR_TX_ONLY, 1U);
[+   1.885s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t t[2] = { 0U, 0U };
[+   1.885s] [host] diff| +    SpiBm_Status_t st[2] = { SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM, SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM };
[+   1.885s] [host] diff| +    sst_fill(s_tx, sst_elem_bytes(bits), 2000U, bits, next_seed());
[+   1.885s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t k = 0U; k < 2U; k++)
[+   1.885s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.885s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t n = (k + 1U) * 1000U;
[+   1.885s] [host] diff| +        (void)SPI_BM_Init(&c);
[+   1.885s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.885s] [host] diff| +        st[k] = SPI_BM_Transfer(spi, s_tx, NULL, n, guard_of(spi, mbr), budget_of(spi, mbr, bits, n));
[+   1.885s] [host] diff| +        t[k] = DWT->CYCCNT - t0;
[+   1.885s] [host] diff| +        (void)SPI_BM_Deinit(spi);
[+   1.885s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.885s] [host] diff| +    /* Oracle: 1000 frames x DSIZE x 2^(MBR+1) kernel ticks, in CPU cycles. */
[+   1.885s] [host] diff| +    const uint64_t num = 1000ULL * bits * (1ULL << (mbr + 1U)) * s_cpu_hz;
[+   1.885s] [host] diff| +    const double exp_c = (double)num / (double)kernel_of(spi);
[+   1.885s] [host] diff| +    const double ctl_c = exp_c * 2.0;            /* the same count with MBR + 1 */
[+   1.885s] [host] diff| +    const double d = (double)(t[1] - t[0]);
[+   1.885s] [host] diff| +    const double err = (d - exp_c) / exp_c;
[+   1.885s] [host] diff| +    const double cerr = (d - ctl_c) / ctl_c;
[+   1.885s] [host] diff| +    const bool ok = (st[0] == SPI_BM_OK) && (st[1] == SPI_BM_OK) && (err <= 0.01) && (err >= -0.01) &&
[+   1.885s] [host] diff| +                    ((cerr > 0.01) || (cerr < -0.01));
[+   1.885s] [host] diff| +    if (!ok)
[+   1.885s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.885s] [host] diff| +        (*bad)++;
[+   1.885s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.885s] [host] diff| +    verdict(ok, "T9 %s TX only MBR /%lu %lu bits: t(2000) - t(1000) = %lu cycles, expected %lu (%+ld ppm, +-1 %%); "
[+   1.885s] [host] diff| +            "with MBR + 1 %lu (%+ld ppm, must be out); st %u %u", name, (unsigned long)(2UL << mbr),
[+   1.885s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)bits, (unsigned long)(t[1] - t[0]), (unsigned long)exp_c, (long)(err * 1e6),
[+   1.885s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)ctl_c, (long)(cerr * 1e6), (unsigned)st[0], (unsigned)st[1]);
[+   1.885s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +/* On this board an SPI master whose SCK is not routed to a pin in its AF never
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +   reaches EOT and receives nothing (F1 development run,
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +   docs/spi_dev_f1_bench_25426f8-dirty.txt: SPI2, SPI3, SPI5 and the pinless SPI1
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +   stopped at CTSIZE = 1, and SPI2 sent 31 frames into an unread RxFIFO without OVR;
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +   the same transfer on SPI4 with its pins in AF5 ended). So every test that needs a
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +   transfer to end runs on an instance the bench routes: SPI6 and SPI1 on J1, SPI4 on
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +   the AD2 bus (owner's decision, 2026-10-03). */
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +static void f1_t9(SPI_TypeDef *spi, const char *name)
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t bad = 0U;
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t m = 0U; m < 2U; m++)
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t b = 0U; b < 2U; b++)
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +            t9_one(spi, name, (m == 0U) ? 3U : 5U, (b == 0U) ? 8U : 16U, &bad);
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    (void)bad;
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +/* ---- T11(a): mode fault on SPI2 (no pin, plan R2-4). No transfer is needed: the
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +   control and the recovery are read with SPI_BM_CheckTransfer() (reads only), and the
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +   fault with SPI_BM_Transfer(), which returns before it starts anything. ---------- */
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +static void f1_t11a(void)
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    SPI_TypeDef *spi = SPI2;
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Config_t c = mkcfg(spi, 7U, 0U, 8U, false, SPI_BM_DIR_TX_ONLY, 1U);
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t g = guard_of(spi, 7U);
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t bud = budget_of(spi, 7U, 8U, 4U);
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    sst_fill(s_tx, 1U, 4U, 8U, next_seed());
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_EnableClock(spi);
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    /* control first: SSI inactive, no fault */
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_Init(&c);
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t s_ctrl = SPI_BM_CheckTransfer(spi, s_tx, NULL, 4U);
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t modf_ctrl = spi->SR & SPI_SR_MODF;
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    /* SSI made active by hand (SSIOP = 0, SSM = 1): mode fault, with SPE = 0 already
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +       (plan revision 2, P1) */
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    spi->CR1 &= ~SPI_CR1_SSI;
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    __DSB();
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t sr_f = spi->SR;
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t c_f = SPI_BM_CheckTransfer(spi, s_tx, NULL, 4U);
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t sr_c = spi->SR;                    /* CheckTransfer writes nothing: MODF stays */
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t s_f = SPI_BM_Transfer(spi, s_tx, NULL, 4U, g, bud);
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t cr1_f = spi->CR1;
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t cfg2_f = spi->CFG2;
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t sr_after = spi->SR;
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t d1 = SPI_BM_Deinit(spi);
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t i1 = SPI_BM_Init(&c);
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t cfg2_rec = spi->CFG2;
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t s_rec = SPI_BM_CheckTransfer(spi, s_tx, NULL, 4U);
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_Deinit(spi);
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    verdict((s_ctrl == SPI_BM_OK) && (modf_ctrl == 0U), "T11(a) control: SSI inactive, CheckTransfer %u, MODF %lu (0)",
[+   1.888s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned)s_ctrl, (unsigned long)(modf_ctrl != 0U));
[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +    verdict(((sr_f & SPI_SR_MODF) != 0U) && (c_f == SPI_BM_ERR_MODF) && ((sr_c & SPI_SR_MODF) != 0U) &&
[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +            (s_f == SPI_BM_ERR_MODF) && ((cr1_f & SPI_CR1_SPE) == 0U) && ((cfg2_f & SPI_CFG2_MASTER) == 0U) &&
[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +            ((sr_after & SPI_SR_MODF) == 0U),
[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +            "T11(a) SSI active by hand: SR 0x%08lX (MODF); CheckTransfer %u (ERR_MODF %u), MODF still set %u; "
[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +            "Transfer %u, then SPE %lu MASTER %lu, MODF cleared %u", (unsigned long)sr_f, (unsigned)c_f,
[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned)SPI_BM_ERR_MODF, (unsigned)((sr_c & SPI_SR_MODF) != 0U), (unsigned)s_f,
[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)((cr1_f & SPI_CR1_SPE) != 0U), (unsigned long)((cfg2_f & SPI_CFG2_MASTER) != 0U),
[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned)((sr_after & SPI_SR_MODF) == 0U));
[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +    verdict((d1 == SPI_BM_OK) && (i1 == SPI_BM_OK) && ((cfg2_rec & SPI_CFG2_MASTER) != 0U) && (s_rec == SPI_BM_OK),

[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +/* ---- T11(b): OVR by hand on a routed instance (full duplex, 4 FIFOs of frames
[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +   pushed, the RxFIFO never read), and TakeErrors. The caller has the pins in AF. ---- */
[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +static void f1_t11b(SPI_TypeDef *spi, const char *name)
[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t n = 4U * fifo_frames(spi, 8U);
[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Config_t f = mkcfg(spi, 2U, 0U, 8U, false, SPI_BM_DIR_FULL_DUPLEX, 1U);
[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_Init(&f);
[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +    spi->CR2 = n;
[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +    spi->CR1 |= SPI_CR1_SPE;
[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t pushed = 0U;
[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +    spi->CR1 |= SPI_CR1_CSTART;

[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +    {

[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +            *(volatile uint8_t *)((uintptr_t)spi + 0x20U) = (uint8_t)pushed;
[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +            pushed++;
[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t sr_b = spi->SR;
[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t taken = SPI_BM_TakeErrors(spi);
[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t sr_a = spi->SR;
[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +    spi->CR1 &= ~SPI_CR1_SPE;
[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_Deinit(spi);

[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +            (taken == (sr_b & (SPI_SR_OVR | SPI_SR_MODF | SPI_SR_TIFRE))) &&
[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +            ((sr_a & (SPI_SR_OVR | SPI_SR_MODF | SPI_SR_TIFRE)) == 0U),
[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +            "T11(b) %s OVR by hand (%lu frames, RxFIFO unread): SR 0x%08lX (EOT, OVR), TakeErrors 0x%03lX, then SR "
[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +            "0x%08lX: taken = the error flags set, all cleared", name, (unsigned long)n, (unsigned long)sr_b,
[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)taken, (unsigned long)sr_a);
[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +}

[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +/* ---- T13: RCC banks. SPI2 transmits from DMA1 stream 1 (circular, 64 B) for 65535
[+   1.890s] [host] diff| +   frames at /256 while CPU2 disables its own bank, then CPU1 disables its bank. ---- */
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +#define T13_STREAM    DMA1_Stream1

[+   1.893s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t r2 = 0U;
[+   1.893s] [host] diff| +    bool m1 = false;
[+   1.893s] [host] diff| +    bool m2 = false;
[+   1.893s] [host] diff| +    if (cm4_send(SST_CMD_T13, 1U))
[+   1.893s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.893s] [host] diff| +        r1 = a->m4_res[2];
[+   1.893s] [host] diff| +        m1 = (a->m4_res[0] == (uint32_t)SPI_BM_OK);
[+   1.893s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.893s] [host] diff| +    delay_ms(30U);
[+   1.893s] [host] diff| +    c1 = ctsize(spi);
[+   1.893s] [host] diff| +    if (cm4_send(SST_CMD_T13, 2U))
[+   1.893s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.893s] [host] diff| +        r2 = a->m4_res[2];
[+   1.893s] [host] diff| +        m2 = (a->m4_res[0] == (uint32_t)SPI_BM_OK) && (a->m4_res[1] == (uint32_t)SPI_BM_OK);
[+   1.893s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.893s] [host] diff| +    delay_ms(30U);
[+   1.893s] [host] diff| +    c2 = ctsize(spi);

[+   1.893s] [host] diff| +    /* CPU1 disables its bank: nobody enables SPI2 now; 30 ms later its count must not
[+   1.893s] [host] diff| +       have moved (read again once the clock is back: an unclocked SPI reads nothing). */
[+   1.893s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t dc = SPI_BM_DisableClock(spi);

[+   1.895s] [host] diff| +    verdict(m2 && run2 && ((r2 & RCC_APB1LENR_SPI2EN) == 0U) && (c1bank_on != 0U),

[+   1.895s] [host] diff| +}

[+   1.895s] [host] diff| +/* ---- CPU2's no-pin tests (T1, T2, T9 on SPI1), printed by CPU1 ----------------------- */

[+   1.895s] [host] diff| +    {

[+   1.895s] [host] diff| +        verdict((r[1] == 0U) && (r[2] == 0U) && (r[3] == 0U) && (r[4] == 1U),

[+   1.895s] [host] diff| +                "I2SCFGR/CRCPOLY/UDRDR off reset %lu; control caught %lu (first difference: config %lu CFG2 0x%08lX/0x%08lX)",

[+   1.895s] [host] diff| +    }

[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +/* T9 on SPI1 (CPU2), J1 pins in AF5 (see the note above f1_t9). */
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +static void f1_m4_t9(void)
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +    SstArea_t *a = SST_AREA;
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t m = 0U; m < 2U; m++)
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t b = 0U; b < 2U; b++)
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t mbr = (m == 0U) ? 3U : 5U;
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t bits = (b == 0U) ? 8U : 16U;
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +            if (cm4_send4(SST_CMD_T9, mbr, bits, 0U, 0U))
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +                const uint32_t d = a->m4_res[1] - a->m4_res[0];
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +                /* CPU2 at 200 MHz, SPI1's kernel at 200 MHz: x1 (plan T9) */
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +                const double exp_c = 1000.0 * (double)bits * (double)(2UL << mbr) * ((double)a->m4_res[2] / (double)s_k123);
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +                const double err = ((double)d - exp_c) / exp_c;
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +                const double cerr = ((double)d - (2.0 * exp_c)) / (2.0 * exp_c);
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +                verdict((a->m4_res[3] == 0U) && (err <= 0.01) && (err >= -0.01) && ((cerr > 0.01) || (cerr < -0.01)),
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +                        "T9 SPI1 (CM4 at %lu Hz) TX only MBR /%lu %lu bits: t(2000) - t(1000) = %lu cycles, expected %lu "
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +                        "(%+ld ppm, +-1 %%); with MBR + 1 %+ld ppm (must be out); status 0x%lX", (unsigned long)a->m4_res[2],
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +                        (unsigned long)(2UL << mbr), (unsigned long)bits, (unsigned long)d, (unsigned long)exp_c,
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +                        (long)(err * 1e6), (long)(cerr * 1e6), (unsigned long)a->m4_res[3]);
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +/* ---- B1 (CPU1): cycles of a cold Init and of a 1-frame Transfer; the caller has the
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +   pins of `spi` in AF (see the note above f1_t9) ---------------------------------- */
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t s_b1_irq_min = 0xFFFFFFFFUL;
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t s_b1_irq_max;
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +static void f1_b1(SPI_TypeDef *spi, const char *name)
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Config_t c = mkcfg(spi, 0U, 0U, 8U, false, SPI_BM_DIR_TX_ONLY, 1U);
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_EnableClock(spi);
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_Deinit(spi);
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +    s_tx[0] = 0x5AU;
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t pm = __get_PRIMASK();
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +    __disable_irq();
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t si = SPI_BM_Init(&c);
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t c_init = DWT->CYCCNT - t0;
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +    t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t st = SPI_BM_Transfer(spi, s_tx, NULL, 1U, guard_of(spi, 0U), budget_of(spi, 0U, 8U, 1U));
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t c_xfer = DWT->CYCCNT - t0;
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +    __set_PRIMASK(pm);
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_Deinit(spi);
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +    LOG("B1 CM7 %s SPI_BM_Init %lu cycles (st %u), SPI_BM_Transfer of 1 frame at /2 %lu cycles (st %u), "
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +        "CalcPrescaler worst %lu", name, (unsigned long)c_init, (unsigned)si, (unsigned long)c_xfer, (unsigned)st,
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +        (unsigned long)s_b1_calc_max);
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +/* The F1_NOPIN step: no pin moves, the host waits for the closing handshake. */
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +static void step_f1_nopin(void)
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +    if (!step_begin("F1_NOPIN"))
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +    f1_t10();
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +    f1_t3();
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +    f1_t1();
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +    f1_t2_caps();
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +    t2_inst(SPI2, "SPI2");
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +    t2_inst(SPI3, "SPI3");
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +    t2_inst(SPI4, "SPI4");
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +    t2_inst(SPI5, "SPI5");
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +    t2_inst(SPI6, "SPI6");
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +    f1_t7d();
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +    f1_t11a();
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +    f1_t13();
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +    f1_m4_nopin();
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +    SPI_TypeDef *const inst[5] = { SPI2, SPI3, SPI4, SPI5, SPI6 };
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +    const char *const names[5] = { "SPI2", "SPI3", "SPI4", "SPI5", "SPI6" };
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < 5U; i++)
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +        t14_check(names[i], inst[i], NULL);
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +    if (!sync0())
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +    step_end();
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +/* ==========================================================================================
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| + * F1 on J1: SPI6 (CPU1, AF8) and SPI1 (CPU2, AF5), the wire PB5 -> PA6 as the oracle
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| + * ========================================================================================== */
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +static const char k_ord[2] = { 'M', 'L' };
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +/* The four J1 pins for SPI6 (S14: SPI6 read back at rest before each AF). SCK pulled
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +   to CPOL (plan "Archivos": pull of SCK = CPOL), MOSI and MISO without pull; `miso`
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +   0 = PA6 (the loop), 1/2 = PB4 with pull-up/down and PA6 in input (plan D2). The
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +   one MISO pin in AF is always switched off before the other is switched on. */
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +static bool spi6_pins(uint32_t cpol, uint32_t miso, uint32_t speed)
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +    s_af_speed = speed;
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +    bool ok = pin_cfg(GPIOA, PA_J1_SCK, GPIO_BM_MODE_AF, SST_AF_SPI6, (cpol != 0U) ? GPIO_BM_PULL_UP : GPIO_BM_PULL_DOWN, 0U) &&
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +              pin_cfg(GPIOB, PB_J1_MOSI, GPIO_BM_MODE_AF, SST_AF_SPI6, GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE, 0U);
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +    if (ok && (miso == 0U))
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +        ok = pin_cfg(GPIOB, PB_J1_MISO, GPIO_BM_MODE_INPUT, 0U, GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE, 0U) &&
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +             pin_cfg(GPIOA, PA_J1_MISO, GPIO_BM_MODE_AF, SST_AF_SPI6, GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE, 0U);
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +    else if (ok)
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +        ok = pin_cfg(GPIOA, PA_J1_MISO, GPIO_BM_MODE_INPUT, 0U, GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE, 0U) &&
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +             pin_cfg(GPIOB, PB_J1_MISO, GPIO_BM_MODE_AF, SST_AF_SPI6, (miso == 1U) ? GPIO_BM_PULL_UP : GPIO_BM_PULL_DOWN, 0U);
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +    s_af_speed = GPIO_BM_SPEED_LOW;
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +    return ok;
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +static bool j1_release(void)
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.898s] [host] diff| +    return j1_inputs(GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE, GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE, GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE, GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE);
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +/* One polling transfer on a CPU1 instance: pattern, Init, Transfer, Deinit (T14 per
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +   transfer), and what the oracles need. The RX buffer starts with a sentinel. */
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +static void xfer_local(const SpiBm_Config_t *c, uint32_t n, uint32_t seed, void *tx, void *rx, SstXferRes_t *r)
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +    SPI_TypeDef *spi = c->spi;
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t bits = c->data_bits;
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t es = sst_elem_bytes(bits);
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +    sst_fill(tx, es, n, bits, seed);
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +    sst_fill_const(rx, es, n, 0xA5A5A5A5UL);
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +    r->init_st = SPI_BM_Init(c);
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +    r->status = SPI_BM_Transfer(spi, tx, rx, n, guard_of(spi, c->mbr), budget_of(spi, c->mbr, bits, n));
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +    r->cycles = DWT->CYCCNT - t0;
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +    r->mism = sst_mismatch(tx, rx, es, n, bits);
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +    r->crc = sst_crc32_frames(rx, es, n, bits);
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +    r->cov = sst_coverage(tx, es, n, bits);
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +    r->irqs = 0U;
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +    r->sentinel_left = 0U;
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +    r->deinit_st = SPI_BM_Deinit(spi);
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +/* One transfer on SPI1, run by CPU2 (SST_CMD_XFER). */
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +static bool xfer_m4(uint32_t xcfg, uint32_t n, uint32_t seed, uint32_t xm, SstXferRes_t *r)
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +    if (!cm4_send4(SST_CMD_XFER, xcfg, seed, n, xm))
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +        return false;
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +    const volatile uint32_t *s = SST_AREA->m4_res;
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +    r->status = s[0];
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +    r->mism = s[1];
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +    r->crc = s[2];
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +    r->cov = s[3];
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +    r->cycles = s[4];
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +    r->irqs = s[5];
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +    r->init_st = s[6];
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +    r->deinit_st = s[7];
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +    r->sentinel_left = s[8];
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +    return true;
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +static bool xfer_ok(const SstXferRes_t *r)
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +    return (r->status == (uint32_t)SPI_BM_OK) && (r->mism == 0U) && (r->init_st == (uint32_t)SPI_BM_OK) &&
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +           (r->deinit_st == (uint32_t)SPI_BM_OK);
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +static void t4_line(const char *name, uint32_t mode, uint32_t bits, uint32_t lsb, uint32_t thr, uint32_t n,
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +                    uint32_t seed, const SstXferRes_t *r)
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +    LOG("T4 %s m%lu b%lu %c t%lu n%lu s=%08lX st=%lu mis=%lu crc=%08lX cov=%08lX cyc=%lu", name, (unsigned long)mode,
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +        (unsigned long)bits, k_ord[lsb], (unsigned long)thr, (unsigned long)n, (unsigned long)seed,
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +        (unsigned long)r->status, (unsigned long)r->mism, (unsigned long)r->crc, (unsigned long)r->cov,
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +        (unsigned long)r->cycles);
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +/* T4: the matrix of the plan on one instance. `m4` = SPI1 through CPU2. */
[+   1.900s] [host] diff| +static void t4_matrix(SPI_TypeDef *spi, const char *name, bool m4, const uint32_t *bl, uint32_t nb)
[+   1.901s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.901s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t mbr = 0U;
[+   1.901s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t sck = 0U;
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_CalcPrescaler(kernel_of(spi), 12500000U, &mbr, &sck);
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t count = 0U;
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t bad = 0U;
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t worst = 0U;
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t mode = 0U; mode <= 3U; mode++)
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +        if (m4)
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +            if (!cm4_send4(SST_CMD_J1PINS, SST_J1P_LOOP, mode >> 1, GPIO_BM_SPEED_HIGH, 0U) || (SST_AREA->m4_res[0] != 1U))
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +                bench_abort("CPU2 J1 pins");
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +                return;
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +        else if (!spi6_pins(mode >> 1, 0U, GPIO_BM_SPEED_HIGH))
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +            return;
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t ib = 0U; ib < nb; ib++)
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t bits = bl[ib];
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t f = fifo_frames(spi, bits);
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t frames[8] = { 1U, 2U, 3U, f - 1U, f, f + 1U, 255U, 4096U };
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t thr[2] = { 1U, thr_max(spi, bits) };
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +            for (uint32_t lsb = 0U; lsb < 2U; lsb++)
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +                for (uint32_t it = 0U; it < 2U; it++)
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +                {
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +                    for (uint32_t k = 0U; k < 8U; k++)
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +                    {
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +                        const uint32_t seed = next_seed();
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +                        SstXferRes_t r;
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +                        if (m4)
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +                        {
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +                            if (!xfer_m4(SST_XCFG(mode, bits, lsb, thr[it], mbr, 0U), frames[k], seed, SST_XM_POLL, &r))
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +                            {
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +                                bench_abort("CPU2 transfer");
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +                                return;
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +                            }
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +                        }
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +                        else
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +                        {
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +                            const SpiBm_Config_t c = mkcfg(spi, mbr, mode, bits, lsb != 0U, SPI_BM_DIR_FULL_DUPLEX, thr[it]);
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +                            xfer_local(&c, frames[k], seed, s_tx, s_rx, &r);
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +                        }
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +                        t4_line(name, mode, bits, lsb, thr[it], frames[k], seed, &r);
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +                        count++;
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +                        if (!xfer_ok(&r))
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +                        {
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +                            bad++;
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +                        }
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +                        if (r.cycles > worst)
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +                        {
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +                            worst = r.cycles;
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +                        }
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +                    }
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +                }
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.902s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +    verdict((bad == 0U) && (count > 0U), "T4 %s loop through J1 at /%lu (%lu Hz): %lu transfers, %lu with a status, a "
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +            "mismatch or a failed Init/Deinit; longest %lu cycles (every one within its max_cycles)", name,
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)(2UL << mbr), (unsigned long)sck, (unsigned long)count, (unsigned long)bad,
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)worst);
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +/* T4's control (plan D2): MISO moved to PB4, pulled up then down: RX all ones / all
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +   zeros. The host computes the mismatches from the seed. */
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +static void t4_control(SPI_TypeDef *spi, const char *name, bool m4, const uint32_t *bl, uint32_t nb)
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t mbr = 0U;
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t sck = 0U;
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_CalcPrescaler(kernel_of(spi), 12500000U, &mbr, &sck);
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t p = 0U; p < 2U; p++)
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +        if (m4)
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +            if (!cm4_send4(SST_CMD_J1PINS, (p == 0U) ? SST_J1P_PB4_UP : SST_J1P_PB4_DOWN, 0U, GPIO_BM_SPEED_HIGH, 0U) ||
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +                (SST_AREA->m4_res[0] != 1U))
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +                bench_abort("CPU2 J1 pins");
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +                return;
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +        else if (!spi6_pins(0U, p + 1U, GPIO_BM_SPEED_HIGH))
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +            return;
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t ib = 0U; ib < nb; ib++)
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t bits = bl[ib];
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t seed = next_seed();
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +            SstXferRes_t r;
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +            if (m4)
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +                (void)xfer_m4(SST_XCFG(0U, bits, 0U, 1U, mbr, 0U), 255U, seed, SST_XM_POLL, &r);
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +            else
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +                const SpiBm_Config_t c = mkcfg(spi, mbr, 0U, bits, false, SPI_BM_DIR_FULL_DUPLEX, 1U);
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +                xfer_local(&c, 255U, seed, s_tx, s_rx, &r);
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +            LOG("T4C %s b%lu %s n255 s=%08lX st=%lu mis=%lu crc=%08lX", name, (unsigned long)bits,
[+   1.903s] [host] diff| +                (p == 0U) ? "up" : "down", (unsigned long)seed, (unsigned long)r.status, (unsigned long)r.mism,
[+   1.904s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)r.crc);
[+   1.904s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.904s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.904s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.904s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.904s] [host] diff| +/* ---- T6: transfers by interrupt (SPI6 here; SPI1's ISR is CPU2's) ------------------- */
[+   1.904s] [host] diff| +static SpiBm_Xfer_t      s_it;
[+   1.904s] [host] diff| +static volatile bool     s_it_active;
[+   1.904s] [host] diff| +static volatile uint32_t s_it_irqs;
[+   1.904s] [host] diff| +static volatile uint32_t s_it_spurious;
[+   1.904s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.904s] [host] diff| +/* The project's vector for the test (plan C14: not the driver's), priority 6. A call
[+   1.904s] [host] diff| +   with no transfer running is the T6(c) control's spurious IRQ: counted, IER cleared. */
[+   1.904s] [host] diff| +void SPI6_IRQHandler(void)
[+   1.904s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.905s] [host] diff| +    s_it_irqs++;
[+   1.905s] [host] diff| +    if (s_it_active)
[+   1.905s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.905s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.905s] [host] diff| +        const bool done = SPI_BM_TransferIrq(SPI6, &s_it);
[+   1.905s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t dt = DWT->CYCCNT - t0;
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +        if (dt < s_b1_irq_min)
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +            s_b1_irq_min = dt;
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +        if (dt > s_b1_irq_max)
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +            s_b1_irq_max = dt;
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +        if (done)
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +            s_it_active = false;
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    else
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +        s_it_spurious++;
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +        SPI6->IER = 0U;
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +static void t6_spi6(void)
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    IRQn_Type irq = SPI6_IRQn;
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_GetIRQn(SPI6, &irq);
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    HAL_NVIC_SetPriority(irq, 6U, 0U);
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    NVIC_ClearPendingIRQ(irq);
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    HAL_NVIC_EnableIRQ(irq);
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t mbr = 4U;                      /* /32: 3.125 MHz */
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t bad = 0U;
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t after_total = 0U;
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t b = 0U; b < 2U; b++)
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t bits = (b == 0U) ? 8U : 16U;
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t es = sst_elem_bytes(bits);
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t frames[3] = { 1U, fifo_frames(SPI6, bits) + 1U, 1024U };
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t k = 0U; k < 3U; k++)
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t n = frames[k];
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t seed = next_seed();
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +            const SpiBm_Config_t c = mkcfg(SPI6, mbr, 0U, bits, false, SPI_BM_DIR_FULL_DUPLEX, 1U);
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +            sst_fill(s_tx, es, n, bits, seed);
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +            sst_fill_const(s_rx, es, n, 0xA5A5A5A5UL);
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +            const SpiBm_Status_t si = SPI_BM_Init(&c);
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +            s_it.tx = s_tx;
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +            s_it.rx = s_rx;
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +            s_it.frames = n;
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +            s_it.guard_cycles = guard_of(SPI6, mbr);
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +            s_it_irqs = 0U;
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +            s_it_spurious = 0U;
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +            s_it_active = true;
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +            const SpiBm_Status_t ss = SPI_BM_TransferStartIt(SPI6, &s_it);
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +            if (ss != SPI_BM_OK)
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +                s_it_active = false;
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t bud = budget_of(SPI6, mbr, bits, n);
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +            while (s_it_active && ((DWT->CYCCNT - t0) < bud))
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +            const bool done = !s_it_active;
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +            s_it_active = false;
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t irqs = s_it_irqs;
[+   1.907s] [host] diff| +            /* (c) ES0445 2.22.3: 10 ms after the end, SPE = 0, no IRQ of SPI6 */
[+   1.907s] [host] diff| +            delay_ms(10U);
[+   1.907s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t after = s_it_irqs - irqs;
[+   1.907s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t spe = SPI6->CR1 & SPI_CR1_SPE;
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +            after_total += after;
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t mism = sst_mismatch(s_tx, s_rx, es, n, bits);
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t crc = sst_crc32_frames(s_rx, es, n, bits);
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +            const SpiBm_Status_t sd = SPI_BM_Deinit(SPI6);
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +            LOG("T6 SPI6 b%lu n%lu s=%08lX st=%lu mis=%lu crc=%08lX irq=%lu after=%lu", (unsigned long)bits,
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)n, (unsigned long)seed, (unsigned long)s_it.status, (unsigned long)mism,
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)crc, (unsigned long)irqs, (unsigned long)after);
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +            if ((si != SPI_BM_OK) || (ss != SPI_BM_OK) || !done || (s_it.status != SPI_BM_OK) || (mism != 0U) ||
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +                (after != 0U) || (spe != 0U) || (sd != SPI_BM_OK))
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +                bad++;
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +    /* The control: TXPIE = 1 by hand with SPE = 0 (TXP is 1 at reset, 53:3597): the IRQ
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +       fires although no transfer runs. */
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Config_t c = mkcfg(SPI6, mbr, 0U, 8U, false, SPI_BM_DIR_FULL_DUPLEX, 1U);
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_Init(&c);
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +    s_it_spurious = 0U;
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +    SPI6->IER = SPI_IER_TXPIE;
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +    delay_ms(1U);
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t spurious = s_it_spurious;
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_Deinit(SPI6);
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +    HAL_NVIC_DisableIRQ(irq);
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +    NVIC_ClearPendingIRQ(irq);
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +    verdict(bad == 0U, "T6 SPI6 by interrupt (8/16 bits, 1, FIFO + 1, 1024 frames): %lu failed; (c) IRQs in the 10 ms "
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +            "after each end, SPE = 0: %lu in total (0)", (unsigned long)bad, (unsigned long)after_total);
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +    verdict(spurious >= 1U, "T6(c) control: TXPIE = 1 by hand with SPE = 0: %lu spurious IRQ (>= 1)",
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)spurious);
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +/* ---- T7(b): SPI6 with BDMA (dma_hal, plan D7), buffers in .d3_ram ----------------- */
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +static DMA_HandleTypeDef s_hbd_rx;
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +static DMA_HandleTypeDef s_hbd_tx;
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +static bool bd_init(DMA_HandleTypeDef *h, BDMA_Channel_TypeDef *ch, uint32_t req, uint32_t dir, uint32_t es)
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +    const DmaHal_Config_t c = {
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +        .instance = ch, .request = req, .direction = dir, .periph_inc = DMA_PINC_DISABLE, .mem_inc = DMA_MINC_ENABLE,
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +        .periph_align = (es == 1U) ? DMA_PDATAALIGN_BYTE : DMA_PDATAALIGN_HALFWORD,
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +        .mem_align = (es == 1U) ? DMA_MDATAALIGN_BYTE : DMA_MDATAALIGN_HALFWORD, .mode = DMA_NORMAL,
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +        .priority = DMA_PRIORITY_HIGH, .fifo_mode = DMA_FIFOMODE_DISABLE, .fifo_threshold = DMA_FIFO_THRESHOLD_FULL,
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +        .mem_burst = DMA_MBURST_SINGLE, .periph_burst = DMA_PBURST_SINGLE, .enable_clock = true, .irq_priority = -1 };
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +    return DMA_HAL_Init(h, &c) == HAL_OK;
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +/* One SPI6 transfer by BDMA, in the order of plan rule 8 and 53:1262-1274. Returns
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +   WaitEnd's status; *sentinel = RX words still holding the sentinel. */
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +static SpiBm_Status_t t7b_one(uint32_t bits, uint32_t n, uint32_t seed, bool rxdma, uint32_t *mism, uint32_t *crc,
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +                              uint32_t *sentinel, bool *steps_ok)
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t es = sst_elem_bytes(bits);
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t mbr = 2U;                      /* /8: 12.5 MHz */
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Config_t c = mkcfg(SPI6, mbr, 0U, bits, false, SPI_BM_DIR_FULL_DUPLEX, 1U);
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    sst_fill(s_bd_tx, es, n, bits, seed);
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    sst_fill_const(s_bd_rx, es, n, 0xA5A5A5A5UL);
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t si = SPI_BM_Init(&c);
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t sp = SPI_BM_DmaPrepare(SPI6, n, rxdma);
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    const bool d_ok = bd_init(&s_hbd_rx, BDMA_Channel0, BDMA_REQUEST_SPI6_RX, DMA_PERIPH_TO_MEMORY, es) &&
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +                      bd_init(&s_hbd_tx, BDMA_Channel1, BDMA_REQUEST_SPI6_TX, DMA_MEMORY_TO_PERIPH, es);
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    bool s_ok = d_ok;
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    if (rxdma && d_ok)
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +        s_ok = HAL_DMA_Start(&s_hbd_rx, (uint32_t)&SPI6->RXDR, (uint32_t)s_bd_rx, n) == HAL_OK;
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    if (s_ok)
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +        s_ok = HAL_DMA_Start(&s_hbd_tx, (uint32_t)s_bd_tx, (uint32_t)&SPI6->TXDR, n) == HAL_OK;
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t ss = SPI_BM_DmaStart(SPI6);
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t sw = SPI_BM_WaitEnd(SPI6, budget_of(SPI6, mbr, bits, n));
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    if (rxdma)
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +        (void)HAL_DMA_PollForTransfer(&s_hbd_rx, HAL_DMA_FULL_TRANSFER, 10U);
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    (void)HAL_DMA_Abort(&s_hbd_tx);                   /* 1. the caller's streams */
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    (void)HAL_DMA_Abort(&s_hbd_rx);
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t se = SPI_BM_DmaEnd(SPI6, guard_of(SPI6, mbr));   /* 2. SPI, 3. DMA bits */
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    (void)DMA_HAL_DeInit(&s_hbd_tx);
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    (void)DMA_HAL_DeInit(&s_hbd_rx);
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t sd = SPI_BM_Deinit(SPI6);
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    *mism = sst_mismatch(s_bd_tx, s_bd_rx, es, n, bits);
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    *crc = sst_crc32_frames(s_bd_rx, es, n, bits);
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t left = 0U;
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < n; i++)
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +        if (sst_get(s_bd_rx, es, i) == (0xA5A5A5A5UL & sst_mask(es * 8U)))
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +            left++;
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    *sentinel = left;
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    *steps_ok = (si == SPI_BM_OK) && (sp == SPI_BM_OK) && s_ok && (ss == SPI_BM_OK) && (se == SPI_BM_OK) &&
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +                (sd == SPI_BM_OK);
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    return sw;
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +static void t7b_spi6(void)
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t bad = 0U;
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t b = 0U; b < 2U; b++)
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t bits = (b == 0U) ? 8U : 16U;
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t frames[4] = { 1U, 9U, 255U, BD_MAXF };
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t k = 0U; k < 4U; k++)
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t seed = next_seed();
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +            uint32_t mism = 0U;
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +            uint32_t crc = 0U;
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +            uint32_t left = 0U;
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +            bool steps = false;
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +            const SpiBm_Status_t st = t7b_one(bits, frames[k], seed, true, &mism, &crc, &left, &steps);
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +            LOG("T7B SPI6 b%lu n%lu s=%08lX st=%u mis=%lu crc=%08lX", (unsigned long)bits, (unsigned long)frames[k],
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)seed, (unsigned)st, (unsigned long)mism, (unsigned long)crc);
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +            if ((st != SPI_BM_OK) || (mism != 0U) || !steps)
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +                bad++;
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t mism = 0U;
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t crc = 0U;
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t left = 0U;
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +    bool steps = false;
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t st = t7b_one(8U, 255U, next_seed(), false, &mism, &crc, &left, &steps);
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +    verdict(bad == 0U, "T7(b) SPI6 by BDMA (channels 0/1, DMAMUX2 11/12, buffers in .d3_ram), 8/16 bits, 1..4096 "
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +            "frames: %lu failed", (unsigned long)bad);
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +    verdict((st == SPI_BM_ERR_OVERRUN) && (left == 255U) && steps,
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +            "T7(b) control RXDMAEN = 0, 255 frames: WaitEnd %u (ERR_OVERRUN %u), RX words with the sentinel %lu (255)",
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned)st, (unsigned)SPI_BM_ERR_OVERRUN, (unsigned long)left);
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +/* ---- T8: speed sweep on J1 (pins at VERY_HIGH) ---------------------------------------- */
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +static void t8_sweep(SPI_TypeDef *spi, const char *name, bool m4, uint32_t wide)
[+   1.913s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.913s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t pass_mask = 0U;     /* bit mbr = both widths passed */
[+   1.913s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t bad_req = 0U;
[+   1.913s] [host] diff| +    if (m4)
[+   1.913s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.913s] [host] diff| +        (void)cm4_send4(SST_CMD_J1PINS, SST_J1P_LOOP, 0U, GPIO_BM_SPEED_VERY_HIGH, 0U);
[+   1.913s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.913s] [host] diff| +    else
[+   1.913s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.913s] [host] diff| +        (void)spi6_pins(0U, 0U, GPIO_BM_SPEED_VERY_HIGH);
[+   1.913s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.913s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < 8U; i++)
[+   1.913s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.913s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t mbr = 7U - i;
[+   1.913s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t f = kernel_of(spi) >> (mbr + 1U);
[+   1.913s] [host] diff| +        bool both = true;
[+   1.913s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t b = 0U; b < 2U; b++)
[+   1.913s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.913s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t bits = (b == 0U) ? 8U : wide;
[+   1.913s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t seed = next_seed();
[+   1.913s] [host] diff| +            SstXferRes_t r;
[+   1.913s] [host] diff| +            if (m4)
[+   1.913s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.913s] [host] diff| +                (void)xfer_m4(SST_XCFG(0U, bits, 0U, 1U, mbr, 0U), 1024U, seed, SST_XM_POLL, &r);
[+   1.913s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.913s] [host] diff| +            else
[+   1.913s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.913s] [host] diff| +                const SpiBm_Config_t c = mkcfg(spi, mbr, 0U, bits, false, SPI_BM_DIR_FULL_DUPLEX, 1U);
[+   1.913s] [host] diff| +                xfer_local(&c, 1024U, seed, s_tx, s_rx, &r);
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +            const bool ok = xfer_ok(&r);
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +            both = both && ok;
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +            LOG("T8 %s /%lu %lu Hz b%lu s=%08lX st=%lu mis=%lu crc=%08lX %s", name, (unsigned long)(2UL << mbr),
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)f, (unsigned long)bits, (unsigned long)seed, (unsigned long)r.status,
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)r.mism, (unsigned long)r.crc, (f <= 25000000U) ? "required" : "recorded");
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +        if (both)
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +            pass_mask |= 1UL << mbr;
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +        else if (f <= 25000000U)
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +            bad_req |= 1UL << mbr;
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t top = 8U;
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t mbr = 0U; mbr < 8U; mbr++)
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +        if ((pass_mask & (1UL << mbr)) != 0U)
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +            top = mbr;
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +            break;
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +    /* The control at the fastest passing speed: MISO on PB4 (pull-up): must fail. */
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +    SstXferRes_t r = { 0U, 0U, 0U, 0U, 0U, 0U, 0U, 0U, 0U };
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +    if (top < 8U)
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +        if (m4)
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +            (void)cm4_send4(SST_CMD_J1PINS, SST_J1P_PB4_UP, 0U, GPIO_BM_SPEED_VERY_HIGH, 0U);
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +            (void)xfer_m4(SST_XCFG(0U, 8U, 0U, 1U, top, 0U), 1024U, next_seed(), SST_XM_POLL, &r);
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +            (void)cm4_send4(SST_CMD_J1PINS, SST_J1P_LOOP, 0U, GPIO_BM_SPEED_HIGH, 0U);
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +        else
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +            (void)spi6_pins(0U, 1U, GPIO_BM_SPEED_VERY_HIGH);
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +            const SpiBm_Config_t c = mkcfg(spi, top, 0U, 8U, false, SPI_BM_DIR_FULL_DUPLEX, 1U);
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +            xfer_local(&c, 1024U, next_seed(), s_tx, s_rx, &r);
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +            (void)spi6_pins(0U, 0U, GPIO_BM_SPEED_HIGH);
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +    verdict(bad_req == 0U, "T8 %s 1024 frames of 8 and %lu bits, MBR /256../2: passed at /%lu and slower (mask 0x%02lX); "
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +            "failed where required (<= 25 MHz) 0x%02lX", name, (unsigned long)wide,
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)((top < 8U) ? (2UL << top) : 0UL), (unsigned long)pass_mask, (unsigned long)bad_req);
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +    verdict((top < 8U) && (r.mism > 0U), "T8 %s control at /%lu, MISO on PB4 (pull-up): %lu mismatches (> 0)", name,
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)((top < 8U) ? (2UL << top) : 0UL), (unsigned long)r.mism);
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +}

[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +/* ---- T12(a): ES0445 2.22.1 on SPI6 ------------------------------------------------------ */
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +static void t12a_spi6(void)
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t mbr = 7U;                      /* /256 */
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Config_t c = mkcfg(SPI6, mbr, 0U, 8U, false, SPI_BM_DIR_FULL_DUPLEX, 1U);
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t g = guard_of(SPI6, mbr);
[+   1.915s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t bud = budget_of(SPI6, mbr, 8U, 1U);
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t timeouts = 0U;
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t data_bad = 0U;
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_Init(&c);
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +    uint8_t *tx = (uint8_t *)s_tx;
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +    uint8_t *rx = (uint8_t *)s_rx;
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < 10000U; i++)
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +        tx[0] = (uint8_t)(i * 37U + 11U);
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +        rx[0] = (uint8_t)~tx[0];
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +        const SpiBm_Status_t st = SPI_BM_Transfer(SPI6, tx, rx, 1U, g, bud);
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +        if (st == SPI_BM_ERR_TIMEOUT)
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +            timeouts++;
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +        if ((st != SPI_BM_OK) || (rx[0] != tx[0]))
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +            data_bad++;
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_Deinit(SPI6);
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +    /* The control (decision 6), by hand: CSTART again with SPE = 1 right after each EOT. */
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_Init(&c);
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +    SPI6->CR2 = 1U;
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +    SPI6->CR1 |= SPI_CR1_SPE;
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +    /* A stall is waited for 20 frame times, then the SPI is restarted (SPE 0 -> 1);
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +       after 20 stalls the control stops: it has shown what it records. */
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t stalls = 0U;
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t runs = 0U;
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; (i < 10000U) && (stalls < 20U); i++)
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +        *(volatile uint8_t *)((uintptr_t)SPI6 + 0x20U) = (uint8_t)i;
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +        SPI6->CR1 |= SPI_CR1_CSTART;
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +        while (((SPI6->SR & SPI_SR_EOT) == 0U) && ((DWT->CYCCNT - t0) < (20U * g * 10U)))
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +        if ((SPI6->SR & SPI_SR_EOT) == 0U)
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +            stalls++;
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +            SPI6->CR1 &= ~SPI_CR1_SPE;
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +            SPI6->IFCR = SST_SPI_IFCR_ALL;
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +            SPI6->CR2 = 1U;
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +            SPI6->CR1 |= SPI_CR1_SPE;
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +        else
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +            (void)*(volatile uint8_t *)((uintptr_t)SPI6 + 0x30U);
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +            SPI6->IFCR = SPI_IFCR_EOTC | SPI_IFCR_TXTFC;
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +        runs++;
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +    SPI6->IER = 0U;
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +    SPI6->CR1 &= ~SPI_CR1_SPE;
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +    SPI6->IFCR = SST_SPI_IFCR_ALL;
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_Deinit(SPI6);
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +    verdict((timeouts == 0U) && (data_bad == 0U), "T12(a) 10000 transfers of 1 frame on SPI6 at /256 without pause: "
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +            "%lu ERR_TIMEOUT, %lu wrong (0, 0)", (unsigned long)timeouts, (unsigned long)data_bad);
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +    log_long("T12(a) control by hand (CSTART again with SPE = 1 right after EOT): %lu transfers, %lu stalls; %s "
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +             "(decision 6: recorded only)", (unsigned long)runs, (unsigned long)stalls,
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +             (stalls > 0U) ? "reproduced" : "not reproduced");
[+   1.917s] [host] diff| +}
